            if ('' !== '')
            {
                location.href = '?&FILTER[' + filer_key + ']=' + $(this).data('v');
            } else {
                location.href = '/?FILTER[' + filer_key + ']=' + $(this).data('v');
            }

        });
    });
    function isInt(n) {
        return n % 1 === 0;
    }
    $('.pagenav a').click(function () {
        if ('' !== '') {
            s_href = '';
            location.href = '/?' + s_href + '&FILTER[PAGE]=' + $(this).text();
        } else {
            location.href = $(this).attr('href');
        }
    });

    $('li.next a').click(function () {
        if ('' !== '') {
            s_href = '';
            location.href = '/?' + s_href + '&FILTER[PAGE]=2';
        } else {
            location.href = $(this).attr('href');
        }
    });

    $('li.prev a').click(function () {
        if ('' !== '') {
            s_href = '';
            location.href = '/?' + s_href + '&FILTER[PAGE]=-1';
        } else {
            location.href = $(this).attr('href');
        }
    });
</script>
<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
    var width = 982;
    var win_w = $(window).width();
    $(window).resize(function(){
        win_w = $(window).width();
        var left = $(this).scrollLeft();
        moveBottomTools(width, win_w, left);
    });
    $(window).scroll(function(){
        var left = $(this).scrollLeft();
        moveBottomTools(width, win_w, left);
    });
    function moveBottomTools(width, win_w, left) {
        if(win_w > width) left -= (win_w-width)/2;
        else left -= 4;
        $('.bottom-tools-holder').css('left',-left);
    }
});
</script>
